### [72] [Efficient Spatio-Temporal Grounding with Multimodal Large Models via Second-Level Tracking and RL Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29023)
*Tianshu Zhang,Yan Wang,Ji Qi,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从帧级追踪转向秒级追踪的流水线，结合跨秒平滑、思维链轨迹合成和强化学习优化，在长视频时空定位中实现效率与质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在长视频上逐帧推理计算昂贵且不稳定，需要在保持定位质量的同时提升效率。

Method: 1) 从帧级追踪降采样到秒级追踪，并引入跨秒平滑保持连续性；2) 利用高级多模态模型合成思维链风格轨迹用于时序定位和目标选择；3) 用真实标注替换生成的时空坐标避免噪声监督；4) 基于 t_IoU+mv_IoU 验证器使用强化学习优化策略。

Result: 在多种FPS设置下，该方法在效率和定位质量之间取得了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的秒级追踪流水线有效降低了长视频推理的计算开销，同时通过思维链监督和强化学习保持了较高的定位精度。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal grounding in long videos requires precise temporal localization and robust object tracking conditioned on natural-language queries. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) show strong reasoning ability, directly applying frame-by-frame inference to long sequences is computationally expensive and unstable. We propose a practical pipeline that shifts from frame-level to second-level tracking and performs cross-second smoothing to preserve continuity while reducing sequence length. To improve reasoning supervision, we synthesize chain-of-thought style trajectories using advanced multimodal models for temporal localization and target selection, and replace generated spatio-temporal coordinates with ground-truth annotations to avoid noisy supervision. We further optimize the policy with reinforcement learning using a verifier based on $t\_\mathrm{IoU}+mv\_\mathrm{IoU}$. Experiments across multiple FPS settings show that our method achieves a strong trade-off between efficiency and localization quality.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Spectrum-Aware Feature Disentangled Network for Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29029)
*Yang Guo,Zihan Yang,Feifei Kou,Yulan Hu,Ran Zhang,Siyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFDNet小目标检测框架，通过自适应频谱解缠（ASD）模块分解骨干网络特征为多个互补频谱分量以消除背景干扰，并利用类原型蒸馏（CPD）增强同类目标的语义一致性，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小目标检测因空间分辨率低、视觉线索弱而极具挑战性，现有方法虽取得进展但不同频谱中的背景干扰仍会降低检测性能，因此需要一种能够有效解缠频谱特征、去除背景干扰的检测框架。

Method: 1) 自适应频谱解缠（ASD）模块：将骨干网络特征分解为多个互补的频谱分量，通过丢弃每个分量中的背景干扰来构建判别性的目标相关表示。2) 类原型蒸馏（CPD）过程：为目标实例建立类原型，通过高效的原型蒸馏强制同类目标形成紧凑的语义表示。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上，SFDNet大幅优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SFDNet通过频谱感知特征解缠和类原型蒸馏，有效解决了小目标检测中的背景干扰和类内语义不一致问题，验证了频谱解缠在小目标检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Small Object Detection (SOD) is a fundamental yet challenging problem in computer vision due to its limited spatial resolution and weak visual cues. Although recent approaches have achieved remarkable advances, the background distractors in different frequency spectra still degrade the performance. In this paper, we propose a novel small object detection framework termed SFDNet, which is capable of detecting small objects via efficient spectrum-aware feature disentanglement. Specifically, we propose an Adaptive Spectrum Disentanglement (ASD) module that decomposes backbone features into multiple complementary spectral components, aiming to construct discriminative object-relevant representations by discarding the background distractors for each component. Afterwards, to strengthen the semantic consistency of the similar objects in the same class, we propose a Class-Wise Prototype Distillation (CPD) procedure, which establishes class prototypes for the object instances and enforces the compact representation by efficient prototype distillation. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks show that SFDNet outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods by a large margin. Our code is available at https://github.com/ManOfStory/SFDNet.

</details>


### [74] [Flow Matching in Feature Space for Stochastic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29059)
*Francois Porcher,Nicolas Carion,Karteek Alahari,Shizhe Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowWM提出了一种在高维预训练特征空间（如DINOv3）内直接进行流匹配的随机世界模型，通过可微的一步投影机制实现高效训练，在准确性和多样性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型难以同时满足两个目标：基于VAE的随机模型在低维重建潜空间中操作，限制了感知性能；而使用强预训练特征的确定性预测器会将多模态未来坍缩为单一模糊均值。

Method: 提出FlowWM，在预训练特征空间（如DINOv3）内直接进行流匹配。针对高维特征空间设计专用方案，引入可微的一步投影机制，实现带有时间一致性和任务驱动目标的高效训练。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界基准FuturePerception上评估，FlowWM在感知性能、模式覆盖率和时间鲁棒性方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: 验证了在高维特征空间中进行随机世界建模的可行性，所提出的设计方案有效提升了世界模型的预测质量。

Abstract: World modeling requires forecasting uncertain futures while preserving information useful for
  downstream perception. Existing visual world models often struggle to satisfy both goals:
  VAE-based stochastic models operate in low-dimensional reconstruction latents, which can
  limit perception performance, while deterministic predictors using strong pretrained features
  collapse multimodal futures into a single blurry mean. In this work, we propose FlowWM, a
  stochastic world model that performs flow matching directly within pretrained feature space
  (e.g., DINOv3). This is challenging because pretrained features are substantially
  high-dimensional, making standard diffusion recipes suboptimal. To address this, we
  investigate the design choices needed for feature-space flow matching and introduce a
  differentiable one-step projection mechanism that enables efficient training with temporal
  consistency and task-driven objectives. We evaluate FlowWM on two benchmarks: a synthetic
  benchmark for systematic evaluation of accuracy and diversity, and a real-world benchmark
  FuturePerception. FlowWM improves perception performance, mode coverage, and horizon
  robustness, validating our proposed design for stochastic world modeling in high-dimensional
  feature spaces.

</details>


### [75] [From Fog Chamber to Aircraft Window: Pixel-Registered Imaging and Synthetic Fine-Tuning Enable Cross-Domain Defogging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29093)
*Alexander Ingold,Sabina D. Menon,Manya Yellepeddy,Alec Ikei,John D. Hodges,Jordan Baker,Syed N. Qadri,Rajesh Menon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种深度去雾流程：先在受控实验室雾环境中预训练，再通过域随机化合成雾进行微调。该方法无需目标域训练即可泛化到多种未见过的场景，包括自由流动雾、iPhone拍摄的飞机舷窗视频等。关键设计：1) 使用单相机雾成像装置生成5114对像素对齐的雾/清晰图像对，支持配对的拉普拉斯比率预测恢复质量和像素级L1重建训练；2) 在Mapillary Vistas上叠加随机化合成雾进行微调。在30种去雾骨干网络中NAFNet表现最佳(24.33dB/0.7912 SSIM)，紧凑替代方案以3%参数达到1.29dB内。飞机窗视频NIQE从6.22降至4.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接解决了真实世界双目去雾中报告的开放迁移限制问题，即如何在无目标域训练的情况下将从受控环境学到的去雾能力泛化到完全未见过的传感器、场景和光路。

Method: 1) 搭建单相机雾成像装置：相机拍摄平板显示器通过114mm散射路径的人造雾室，生成5114对像素对齐的雾/清晰图像。使用配对的拉普拉斯比率作为比单图像代理更好的恢复质量预测指标(Spearman ρ=0.632 vs 0.399)，并支持像素精确L1重建训练避免对抗幻觉。2) 在Mapillary Vistas数据集上叠加域随机化的合成雾（变化强度、空间变化、大气光和噪声条件）进行微调。

Result: 在552张保留测试集上比较30种去雾骨干网络，NAFNet最优(24.33dB/0.7912 SSIM)；紧凑替代方案以3%参数达到1.29dB内。ResNet-50分类器确认恢复保留了语义内容。飞机窗视频NIQE从6.22降至4.97，全运动序列输出时间稳定。在O-HAZE/NH-HAZE分割上达到20.71dB/0.683 SSIM。

Conclusion: 提出的实验室到野外去雾流程能够有效泛化到多种未见过的分布外场景，解决了实际双目去雾的迁移限制问题。像素对齐的配对数据与域随机化合成微调相结合是实现泛化的关键。

Abstract: A deep defogging pipeline pretrained on controlled laboratory fog and fine-tuned with domain-randomized synthetic fog applied to clear outdoor scenes generalizes across a graded sequence of out-of-distribution settings with no target-domain training, from chamber-free free-flowing fog to iPhone video recorded through an aircraft cabin window in flight, an entirely unseen sensor, scene, and optical path. This directly addresses an open transfer limitation reported for real-world binocular defogging. Two design choices support the transfer. First, a single-camera fog imager photographs a flat-panel display through an artificial-fog enclosure with a fixed 114~mm scattering path, producing 5{,}495 pixel-aligned foggy/clear pairs. Exact registration permits a paired Laplacian ratio that predicts per-image restoration quality far better than single-image proxies (Spearman $ρ= 0.632$ versus $0.399$) and supports pixel-exact $L_1$ reconstruction training that avoids adversarial hallucination. Second, the fog-chamber checkpoint is fine-tuned on Mapillary Vistas crops overlaid with on-the-fly randomized synthetic fog spanning a broad range of strengths, spatial variations, airlights, and noise conditions. On a 552-image held-out split, a uniform comparison of 30 restoration backbones places NAFNet at the top (24.33~dB~/~0.7912~SSIM), with a compact alternative within 1.29~dB at 3\% of the parameter count, and a ResNet-50 classifier confirms that the restoration preserves semantic content rather than only pixel-level structure. On unpaired aircraft-window video, NIQE decreases from a mean of 6.22 to 4.97 after fine-tuning, with temporally stable output across full-motion sequences. The same backbone, under paired supervision, also reaches 20.71~dB~/~0.683~SSIM on a non-overlapping O-HAZE/NH-HAZE split (a transferability check rather than a competitive ranking).

</details>


### [76] [HorizonRelight: Relighting Long-horizon Videos Consistently via Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29095)
*Jing Yang,Mayoore Jaiswal,Zian Wang,Steven Zeng,Rochelle Pereira,Yajie Zhao,Jianyuan Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的长视频重光照方法，通过将长时间重光照重新定义为时间条件化的潜域翻译，利用掩码目标域自条件化和暖启动提示技术，解决了分块滑动窗口推理带来的时间不连续性问题，显著提升了长视频的时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频重光照方法在长视频上采用分块滑动窗口推理，导致分块边界处出现时间不连续和伪影，缺乏有效的跨块一致性约束。

Method: 1) 将长视频重光照重新定义为时间条件化的潜域翻译；2) 通过跨边界传播目标域隐变量实现连续性，并采用掩码目标域自条件化使其可学习；3) 引入暖启动提示，使用可控生成模型的已重光照提示锚点初始化目标域状态。

Result: 在真实世界长视频上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了时间一致性，大幅减少了分块边界伪影，并有效抑制了跨块的不必要外观变化。

Conclusion: 本文通过潜域翻译框架、掩码自条件化和暖启动提示，有效解决了扩散模型长视频重光照的时间不一致问题，为基于提示的重光照提供了通用接口。

Abstract: Diffusion-based video relighting enables controllable relighting from a single input video, but modern video diffusion backbones are trained on short clips and applied to long-horizon videos through chunked sliding-window inference, often causing temporal discontinuities at chunk boundaries. We address this by reframing long-horizon relighting as \emph{temporally conditioned latent domain translation}. Our framework enforces cross-chunk continuity by propagating target-domain latents across boundaries and makes this behavior learnable using \emph{masked target-domain self-conditioning}, training the model to continue from temporally masked propagated context. We further introduce \emph{warm-start prompting} with a relit prompt anchor from a controllable generative model, which establishes the initial target-domain state and creates a general interface for prompt-based relighting. Experiments on in-the-wild long-horizon videos show markedly improved temporal consistency, with chunk-boundary artifacts largely reduced and unwanted appearance changes across chunks greatly suppressed.

</details>


### [77] [TrafficAlign: Aligning Large Language Models for Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29097)
*Zhi Tu,Liangkun Niu,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrafficAlign是一个基于真实驾驶视频自动生成交通场景的框架，能够对齐LLM与真实交通分布，生成的场景可暴露更多自动驾驶模型碰撞风险，并用于微调模型显著降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的大语言模型生成的交通场景与真实世界的交通分布不一致，导致生成的场景缺乏现实有效性，无法真实反映自动驾驶系统在实际道路中的表现。

Method: 提出TrafficAlign框架，包含三个关键步骤：1) 基于真实驾驶视频自动合成交通场景；2) 对生成场景进行数据验证；3) 使用合成场景对齐大语言模型，使其生成更符合真实分布的交通场景。

Result: TrafficAlign生成的场景在三个自动驾驶模型上比最先进方法平均多暴露10.8%的碰撞；使用这些场景微调后，驾驶模型的碰撞率降低36.1%；在六个地理区域的定性研究中，生成场景与对应区域交通分布高度对齐。

Conclusion: TrafficAlign有效提升了生成场景的真实性和实用性，既能作为自动驾驶模型的压力测试工具，也能通过数据增强提升模型的安全性，且具有良好的地理泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent research has investigated the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate traffic scenarios for autonomous driving. However, pretrained LLMs often fail to align with real-world traffic distributions. In this work, we present TrafficAlign, an automated framework that synthesizes traffic scenarios based on real-world driving videos, performs data validation, and aligns LLMs with the synthesized scenarios. The evaluation shows that traffic scenarios generated by TrafficAlign are highly effective, revealing up to 10.8% more collisions on average across three autonomous driving models than state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, fine-tuning these driving models with TrafficAlign-generated scenarios significantly reduced collision rates by 36.1% compared with the original models. A qualitative study using traffic datasets from six geographically diverse regions shows that TrafficAlign-generated scenarios exhibit strong alignment with corresponding traffic distributions in these regions.

</details>


### [78] [BTI-Net: Bidirectional Decoder-Level Task Interaction via Uncertainty-Aware Gating for Multi-Task Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29102)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Md Kawsar Mahmud Khan Zunayed,Safin Ahmmed,Sk Imran Hossain,Engelbert Mephu Nguifo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BTI-Net通过任务交互模块(TIM)和不确定性代理注意力(UPA)实现医学图像分割与分类的双向通信，在多个医学基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器共享架构限制了解码器仅能重构任务私有表征，永久丢弃了各分支可相互提供的边界线索和语义先验，缺乏跨任务协同。

Method: 提出BTI-Net，通过任务交互模块(TIM)在每个解码器层级建立双向并行通路，将空间边界上下文门控输入分类分支，全局语义先验乘性调节分割解码器，特征从粗语义到细边界逐级传播；并提出不确定性代理注意力(UPA)，利用跨任务对齐、场景复杂度和预测置信度三个信号对每个TIM输出进行实例级和层级自适应门控。

Result: 在超声、皮肤镜和脑MRI三个医学基准上，分割IoU和分类精度均优于编码器共享和解码器交互基线；消融实验显示自适应门控比固定双向交互提升+2.36 IoU，分类精度比最强多任务基线提升最高+2.26点。

Conclusion: BTI-Net验证了双向跨任务通信和自适应不确定性门控的有效性，UPA可作为可靠的单通路任务失败信号，无需随机采样的额外开销。

Abstract: Jointly learning to segment and classify medical images demands cross-task synergy, yet encoder-sharing architectures limit decoder reconstruction to task-private representations, permanently discarding the boundary cues and semantic priors each branch could supply to the other. This work introduces BTI-Net, which establishes bidirectional communication at every decoder level through two parallel pathways via Task Interaction Modules (TIM). Spatial boundary context is gated into the classification branch, while global semantic priors multiplicatively modulate the decoder, with refined features propagating progressively from coarse semantics to fine boundary detail across all four decoder resolutions. Since cross-task interaction is not equally reliable for every input, Uncertainty Proxy Attention (UPA) gates each TIM output per instance and per level using three signals that capture cross-task alignment, scene complexity, and prediction confidence, without external annotations or additional inference passes. Experiments on three medical benchmarks spanning ultrasound, dermoscopy, and brain MRI demonstrate consistent improvements in segmentation IoU and classification accuracy over both encoder-sharing and decoder-interaction baselines. Ablation confirms adaptive gating contributes +2.36 IoU over fixed bidirectional interaction, and classification accuracy improves by up to +2.26 points over the strongest multi-task baseline. UPA's uncertainty proxies serve as reliable single-pass task-failure signals without the overhead of stochastic sampling. Code: https://github.com/C-loud-Nine/BTI-Net_MTL

</details>


### [79] [A Deep Multiscale Neural Network for Accurate Neurological Disorder Detection from MRI Scans and Real-Time Web Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29106)
*Ali Fatahi,Hoda Zamani,Mohammad H. Nadimi-Shahraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出End-Net，一种用于多类MRI神经疾病分类的24层卷积网络，包含21个优化Inception模块，通过WGAN-GP数据增强和随机欠采样处理类别不平衡，在多类神经疾病数据集上超越现有模型，并集成到在线实时推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度CNN大多针对二分类任务优化，难以捕捉多类特征以区分不同神经疾病的细微解剖差异，亟需一种高效的多类MRI分类模型。

Method: End-Net包含24个卷积层，起始为卷积块，后接21个优化Inception模块，通过并行1×1、3×3、分解5×5卷积及最大池化提取多尺度特征；使用全局平均池化、紧凑全连接分类器和dropout减少参数、防止过拟合；采用WGAN-GP增强少数类并随机欠采样多数类以处理类别不平衡。

Result: 在多类神经疾病数据集（阿尔茨海默病、脑肿瘤、多发性硬化症及健康对照）上，End-Net在准确率和泛化能力上均优于现有架构。

Conclusion: End-Net有效实现了多类神经疾病的MRI分类，并可通过在线系统支持实时网络推理，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Neurological disorders involve diverse pathologies of the brain and nervous system, making early and accurate detection essential. While many deep CNNs have been developed for MRI-based classification of neurological disorders, most are optimized for binary tasks and often fail to capture the multi-class features needed to distinguish subtle anatomical differences across conditions. This study proposes the Enhanced Neurological Disorder Detection Network (End-Net) for multi-class MRI classification of neurological disorders. End-Net includes 24 convolutional layers, beginning with convolutional blocks followed by 21 optimized inception modules. These modules extract multiscale features via parallel 1 x 1, 3 x 3, and factorized 5 x 5 convolutional branches, along with max pooling, enabling the model to capture complementary texture, edge, shape, and contextual information. A global average pooling head, compact fully connected classifier, and dropout reduce parameters, limit overfitting, and improve robustness. End-Net was evaluated on the Multi-Class Neurological Disorder dataset, comprising MRI scans from patients with Alzheimer's disease, brain tumors, multiple sclerosis, and healthy controls. Severe class imbalance was addressed by augmenting minority classes with WGAN-GP and randomly undersampling the majority class. The results show that End-Net outperforms existing architectures in both accuracy and generalization. The model is also integrated into an online system for real-time web-based inference and accessibility.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Backscatter: AlphaEarth Land-Cover Priors for Rapid SAR Flood Segmentation Across Foundation Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29134)
*Sanjay Thasma,Yu-Hsuan Ho,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在无双时相SAR数据条件下，利用稳定陆地表层先验知识（DEM和AlphaEarth）增强单时相SAR洪水分割性能，发现两种先验均优于纯SAR基线，且AlphaEarth在困难事件上表现更优、DEM更稳定，为应急洪水制图提供了互补方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学影像在重大洪灾中常不可用，单时相SAR难以区分新增淹没区、永久水体及其他平滑表面，亟需无需配准的前后时相数据即可准确进行洪水分割的方法。

Method: 使用CONUS区域ImpactMesh-Flood数据集，对比四种骨干网络（从头训练CNN UNet、ImageNet预训练UNet、SAR预训练TerraMind ViT、光学卫星预训练DINOv3 ViT），在SAR-only、SAR+DEM、SAR+AlphaEarth三种配置下，采用相同融合设计、训练协议和事件分层划分，对飓风Florence和路易斯安那洪水两个留出事件进行评价。

Result: 两种辅助先验在所有骨干网络和测试事件上均优于纯SAR基线；AlphaEarth在更困难的Florence事件上超越DEM并取得最佳IoU，DEM在路易斯安那事件上最优且更稳定；种子分析揭示AlphaEarth峰值性能和召回率更高但稳定性较低。

Conclusion: 单时相SAR洪水分割可重构为雷达观测与稳定陆表先验的对齐任务，学习型（AlphaEarth）和物理（DEM）先验提供了相互补充的途径，需按事件分别评估模型性能。

Abstract: Rapid flood mapping is critical for emergency response, yet optical imagery is often unusable during major flooding and single-temporal SAR is ambiguous, since new inundation, permanent water, and other smooth surfaces produce similar backscatter. This study evaluates whether stable land-context priors can improve post-event SAR flood segmentation when a registered, seasonally matched pre-event acquisition is unavailable. Using the CONUS (Continental United States) subset of ImpactMesh-Flood, we compare four backbones spanning distinct pretraining regimes-a from-scratch CNN UNet, an ImageNet-pretrained UNet, the SAR-pretrained TerraMind Vision Transformer, and the optical-satellite-pretrained DINOv3 Vision Transformer-in SAR-only, SAR+DEM, and SAR+AlphaEarth configurations under an identical fusion design, training protocol, and event-stratified split. Models are selected on a validation flood event and evaluated separately on two held-out events, Hurricane Florence and the Louisiana floods, with three-seed reporting for auxiliary configurations. Both auxiliary priors improve over the observed SAR-only baselines across all backbones and test events. AlphaEarth exceeds DEM on the harder Florence event for every backbone and achieves the best Florence IoU, while DEM is competitive on Louisiana and produces the best result there. The seed analysis reveals a trade-off: DEM is more stable across initializations, whereas AlphaEarth offers higher peak performance and higher recall on the harder event. Cross-event differences track flood-class prevalence and similarity to the training distribution, underscoring the need for per-event evaluation. We reframe single-temporal SAR flood segmentation as an alignment between radar observations and stable land-surface priors, where learned and physical context offer complementary pathways to more reliable rapid flood mapping.

</details>


### [81] [CMTFormer: Marrying Transformer with Hierarchical Information Interaction for RGB-Event Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29136)
*Yu Li,Yuenan Hou,Yingmei Wei,Jiangming Chen,Yanming Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CMTFormer，一种分层集成RGB和事件流信息的跨模态交互Transformer，通过浅层对齐模块(SAM)、中层跨模态增强模块(CEM)和深层可学习融合模块(LDFM)实现逐步精细的RGB-事件融合，在DSEC-Detection和PKU-DAVIS-SOD基准上超越现有单模态和多模态检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机捕捉稀疏亮度变化，具有高时间分辨率和动态范围，弥补了传统RGB帧的不足。但现有多模态融合技术难以处理RGB帧与事件流之间的固有异质性，易导致跨模态融合中的噪声放大或冗余特征集成。

Method: 提出CMTFormer，包含三个分层交互模块：浅层对齐模块(SAM)实现RGB和事件低级特征的高效融合，缓解属性差异并防止噪声信息；中层跨模态增强模块(CEM)利用纹理和边缘信息产生相互增强的中级特征；深层可学习融合模块(LDFM)通过可学习权重进行高级信息聚合，实现自适应融合。另设计空间先验模块利用全局空间信息增强定位精度。

Result: 在DSEC-Detection和PKU-DAVIS-SOD两个事件相机目标检测基准上，CMTFormer在单模态和多模态设置下均一致超越现有检测方法。

Conclusion: 分层跨模态交互策略能有效处理RGB-事件异质性，实现高效稳定的多模态协作，代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Event cameras capture sparse brightness changes with high temporal resolution and high dynamic range, compensating for the deficiencies of the conventional RGB frames. However, previous multi-modal fusion techniques typically fail to handle the inherent heterogeneity between RGB frames and event streams, thus easily leading to noise amplification or redundant feature integration during cross-modal fusion. In this paper, we propose a Cross-Modal information inTeraction transFormer, coined as CMTFormer, which hierarchically integrates RGB and event information to achieve efficient and stable multimodal collaboration. Specifically, we design a shallow-to-deep information interaction scheme. In the shallow stage, we present the Shallow Alignment Module (SAM) to achieve an efficient fusion of RGB and event low-level features, which mitigates attribute disparities and prevents noisy information. In the middle stage, we devise the Cross-modal Enhancement Module (CEM) that utilizes texture and edge information to produce mutually reinforced middle-level features. In the deep stage, we present the Learnable Deep Fusion Module (LDFM) which performs high-level information aggregation through learnable weights, thus enabling the network to adaptively fuse RGB and event clues. A Spatial Prior Module is further designed to utilize global spatial information to enhance localization accuracy. Extensive experiments are conducted on two prevalent event-based object detection benchmarks, i.e., DSEC-Detection and PKU-DAVIS-SOD. Our CMTFormer consistently surpasses the detection counterparts in both uni-modal and multi-modal settings, strongly demonstrating the effectiveness of our paradigm. Codes will be available upon publication.

</details>


### [82] [GPC: Large-Scale Generative Pretraining for Transferable Motor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29148)
*Yi Shi,Yifeng Jiang,Chen Tessler,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式预训练控制器（GPC），通过将运动数据分词化为离散代码并采用GPT风格的自回归Transformer进行下一标记预测，结合端到端强化学习联合优化运动词汇和控制策略，实现物理角色动画的通用、鲁棒且可复用的生成式控制，在大量运动片段上达到99.98%的成功复现率，并展现出自然的应急行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现物理角色动画在广泛任务中能够以自然、逼真的方式完成控制是实际应用的关键挑战，现有方法在训练复杂度、控制器的通用性和可复用性上存在局限。

Method: 采用端到端强化学习联合优化基于有限标量量化（FSQ）的"运动词汇表"和对应的控制策略；学习词汇表后，用GPT风格的自回归Transformer建模离散代码的结构；提出一套适应技术用于微调控制器以适应新的下游任务。

Result: 在大量运动片段复现上达到99.98%的成功率；控制器展现出自然的应急行为，如对扰动的响应和跌倒后的恢复行为；高度鲁棒，适用于多种下游应用。

Conclusion: GPC框架大幅简化了训练过程，相比以往基于标记化的方法更高效，生成了通用、鲁棒且可复用的生成式控制器，为物理角色动画的实用化提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Developing controllers capable of completing a wide range of tasks in a natural and life-like manner is a key challenge in enabling practical applications of physics-based character animation. In this work, we introduce Generative Pretrained Controllers (GPC), which leverage tokenization and next-token modeling to create general-purpose, reusable generative controllers from large-scale motion datasets. Our framework utilizes end-to-end reinforcement learning to jointly optimize a "motion vocabulary", modeled via Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ), along with a corresponding control policy that can map the discrete codes to physics-based controls. After the "codebook" has been learned, the underlying structure of this large vocabulary is modeled by training a GPT-style autoregressive transformer, leading to a powerful generative controller that generates controls for a physically simulated character by performing next-token prediction. Once the generative controller has been trained, we propose a suite of adaptation techniques for finetuning the controller for new downstream tasks. Our proposed framework greatly simplifies the training process compared to previous tokenized methods, and achieves a 99.98% success rate in reproducing a vast corpus of motion clips. The generative controller exhibits a variety of natural emergent behaviors, such as responsive behaviors to perturbations and recovery behaviors after falling. This results in highly robust general purpose controllers for a variety of downstream applications.

</details>


### [83] [Spatially Localized Image Degradation Embeddings for Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29162)
*Krishna Srikar Durbha,Hassene Tmar,Ping-Hao Wu,Ioannis Katsavounidis,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIDE-IQA，一种通过双分支ViT框架和阈值边界排除机制进行空间局部化退化嵌入的无参考图像质量评估方法，有效解决了现有自监督学习对空间局部退化敏感度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL方法在NR-IQA中均匀施加合成退化，对真实世界中空间局部化及共现退化敏感度不足，存在表征盲区。

Method: 提出SLIDE-IQA框架：1）双分支Vision Transformer架构；2）将空间有界退化注入对比预训练目标；3）引入阈值边界排除机制（Threshold-Bounded Exclusion Mechanism）解决空间局部化失真带来的结构冲突。

Result: SLIDE-IQA的纯合成预训练显著提升了对局部化失真的敏感度，在NR-IQA基准测试上达到与现有SSL NR-IQA模型竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 通过空间局部化的退化嵌入设计，弥补了SSL在NR-IQA中的表征盲区，验证了合成退化预训练在提升局部失真感知方面的有效性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) currently drives state-of-the-art performance in no-reference image quality assessment (NR-IQA). However, standard SSL pipelines uniformly apply synthetic distortions across the entire image field, which can limit their sensitivity to spatially localized and co-occurring degradations encountered in real-world content. In this work, we empirically expose this representational blind spot across existing state-of-the-art encoders, demonstrating their reduced sensitivity to spatially bounded image degradations. To bridge this gap, we introduce Spatial Localized Image Degradation Embeddings for Image Quality Assessment (SLIDE-IQA). SLIDE-IQA employs a dual-branch Vision Transformer framework that injects spatially bounded degradations into a contrastive pretraining objective. To handle the spatial complexity of these degradations, we introduce a Threshold-Bounded Exclusion Mechanism, a representational design choice that resolves structural conflicts arising from spatially localized distortions to ensure the latent space respects both degradation type and spatial scale. Finally, we show that SLIDE-IQA's synthetic-only pretraining significantly improves sensitivity to localized distortions, while achieving competitive performance on NR-IQA benchmarks against existing SSL NR-IQA models.

</details>


### [84] [Articulating then Matching: Zero-Shot Shape Matching for Uncurated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29167)
*Qilong Liu,Qinfeng Xiao,Chenyuan Yi,Liying Zhang,Kit-lun Yick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATM是一个零样本的3D形状密集对应框架，通过"articulate-then-match"范式，利用预训练视觉基础模型和参数化形状先验，从多视角渲染中估计参数化模型，将不同输入映射到共享规范参数空间建立鲁棒的粗对应，再通过谱精化得到精确稠密对应，无需对应训练数据，天然免疫拓扑噪声，能处理网格、点云、3D高斯等多种模态，在非等距基准上减少73%(TOPKIDS)和37%(SMAL)误差，对高达20万顶点的原始扫描保持近常数计算时间和一致优越精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的3D形状密集对应面临缺乏训练时间和样本、数据存在拓扑扭曲的高分辨率问题、以及需要处理多种3D表示形式的挑战，现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 采用articulate-then-match范式：1) 利用预训练视觉基础模型和参数化形状先验从多视角渲染估计参数模型；2) 通过多视角几何一致性进行系统性优化；3) 将多样输入映射到共享规范参数空间建立粗对应；4) 通过谱精化得到精确稠密映射。

Result: 在非等距基准上，平均测地误差达到2.4(TOPKIDS)和3.8(SMAL)，相比URSSM基线分别减少73%和37%；在高达20万顶点的原始扫描上保持近常数计算时间和一致优越精度。

Conclusion: ATM无需对应训练数据，天然免疫于拓扑噪声，能无缝处理多种3D模态，在真实世界非等距形状对应任务上表现优异且鲁棒。

Abstract: Finding dense correspondences between 3D shapes is a fundamental yet unresolved challenge, especially in real-world environments. These environments present severe challenges, including the lack of time and sufficient samples for training, the prevalence of uncurated extreme-high resolution data with topological distortions, and the need to handle diverse 3D representations. In this paper, we present ATM, a zero-shot framework that requires no correspondence-specific training and robustly addresses these issues at once through an articulate-then-match paradigm. Rather than relying on intrinsic geometric properties, we leverage powerful pretrained vision foundation models and parametric shape priors to estimate parametric shape models from multi-view renderings, and systematically ground these estimations via multi-view geometric consistency. By mapping diverse inputs into a shared canonical parametric space, we inherently establish robust coarse correspondences that bypass topological noise, which are then refined into precise dense mappings via spectral refinement. Operating purely on test-time optimized parametric reconstructions, ATM requires no correspondence training data, is naturally immune to connectivity artifacts, and seamlessly handles diverse 3D modalities, including meshes, point clouds, and 3D Gaussians. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong results on non-isometric benchmarks (average geodesic errors of 2.4-TOPKIDS, 3.8-SMAL), reducing errors by 73% and 37% respectively compared to the baseline URSSM. Furthermore, it exhibits unprecedented robustness on in-the-wild raw scans of up to 200k vertices per shape while maintaining near-constant computation time and consistent superior accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Anomaly Factory 3D: A Modular Framework for Diverse Pseudo-Anomaly Synthesis in Unsupervised 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29181)
*Ali Balapour,Faraz Hach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AF3AD是一个模块化框架，能从正常点云合成多样化的伪异常数据，用于扩展无监督3D异常检测方法的训练数据，在AnomalyShapeNet和Real3D-AD数据集上显著提升了检测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云中的缺陷检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本稀少且多样，训练通常仅限于正常数据。现有方法缺乏生成多样化伪异常的通用工具。

Method: 提出Anomaly Factory 3D (AF3AD)，一个模块化框架，使用中心条件参数化变形模型，在局部PCA框架中定义，包含核控制的空间衰减、各向异性、方向门控以及法向/切向位移场，支持多种几何缺陷预设。

Result: 在AnomalyShapeNet和Real3D-AD上，AF3AD在对象级和点级的检测与定位上均取得一致改进，且对噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AF3AD是一个独立的合成工具，易于使用且能跨检测范式迁移，有望广泛应用于不同的3D异常检测方法。

Abstract: Detecting and localizing defects in 3D point clouds is challenging because abnormal samples are scarce and diverse, while training is often limited to normal data. We propose Anomaly Factory 3D (AF3AD), a modular framework that synthesizes diverse pseudo-anomalies from normal point clouds to expand the training data for unsupervised 3D anomaly detection methods that rely on pseudo-anomalies. AF3AD uses a center-conditioned parametric deformation model defined in local PCA frames, with kernel-controlled spatial falloff, anisotropy, directional gating, and normal/tangential displacement fields, enabling a broad set of geometric defect presets. We demonstrate its ease-of-use and effectiveness by integrating AF3AD with an offset-prediction detector and a reconstruction-based anomaly detection method, showing that AF3AD transfers across detection paradigms. Experiments on AnomalyShapeNet and Real3D-AD show consistent improvements in object- and point-level detection and localization, supported by ablations on preset groups and robustness under noise. AF3AD is designed as a standalone synthesis tool to facilitate adoption across different 3D anomaly detection paradigms. Code is available at github.com/vpc-ccg/AF3AD.

</details>


### [86] [DTI: Dynamic Trajectory Initialization for Generative Face Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29198)
*Yingwei Tang,Chen Yan,Wendi Liu,Qiang Hu,Xiaoyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态轨迹初始化（DTI）范式，将生成式人脸视频超分辨率（GFVSR）从全生成过程重构为输入驱动的定向修复，通过增强-注入条件机制和判别性引导（DG）实现了高保真度与高感知质量的平衡，仅需少量微调即可达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GFVSR方法依赖预训练扩散模型的生成先验，但存在固定采样、推理成本高、缺乏大规模辅助训练时效果差的问题，且过度追求感知指标导致保真度低。

Method: 1) DTI范式：将GFVSR重新表述为输入驱动的定向修复；2) 增强-注入条件机制：对预训练DiT骨干进行增强与注入，提升保真度；3) 判别性引导（DG）：通过信噪比（SNR）对齐目标训练，动态设置起始采样点。

Result: 仅需少量模型适配和微调，在多种指标和基准上达到SOTA性能。通过分析实际综合质量与常见指标的关系，验证了感知-失真权衡，并指出LPIPS是最具说服力的指标。

Conclusion: DTI范式有效解决了GFVSR中感知质量与保真度的矛盾，以轻量级微调实现了高效且高质量的FVSR，为生成式超分辨率提供了新方向。

Abstract: As the most perceptually powerful Face Video Super-Resolution (FVSR) method, existing works in Generative FVSR (GFVSR) mainly exploit the generative prior of pretrained diffusion models. However, viewed as full generation, they suffer from fixed sampling and expensive inference costs if without large-scale auxiliary training. Furthermore, an excessive pursuit of generic perceptual metrics often results in low fidelity. To address these issues, we present Dynamic Trajectory Initialization (DTI) paradigm for GFVSR, which reformulates GFVSR as an input-driven directional restoration. With a novel enhancement-and-injection conditioning mechanism for pretrained DiT backbone, fidelity of our model has been significantly improved without compromising perceptual quality. To dynamically set the starting sampling point, we propose a Discriminative Guide (DG) trained via objective Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) alignment. With only minor model adaptation and fine-tuning, our method achieves a SOTA overall performance across diverse metrics and benchmarks. An analysis of relationship between actual comprehensive quality and common metrics is also conducted, which demonstrates the perception-distortion trade-off and that the LPIPS is the most convincing metric in our case.

</details>


### [87] [Zero-Gated Language-conditioned Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29208)
*Guanhui Qiao,Lu Zhou,Ding Jiang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZGL是一个轻量级语言条件预测器，通过将观测动作的文本描述作为语义先验注入DCT时空Transformer，在保持强运动主干的同时提升3D人体运动预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动历史提供了3D人体运动预测的核心运动学证据，但缺乏显式的高层语义指导。现有方法仅依赖姿态序列，未能利用语义信息来辅助预测。

Method: 1) 将观测姿态渲染后使用视觉-语言模型生成单句描述；2) 用冻结的CLIP-L文本编码器编码描述；3) 将编码结果投影为少量条件令牌；4) 通过带有零门控的紧凑交叉注意力适配器将条件令牌注入DCT时空Transformer，初始化时门控为零使网络与纯姿态基线数值一致。

Result: 在Human3.6M上，ZGL在整体MPJPE指标上优于代表性运动预测基线；在CMUMocap上的结果进一步表明，紧凑的标题条件可迁移到第二个基准数据集。

Conclusion: 语言条件作为语义先验能够有效提升3D人体运动预测性能，且通过零门控适配器设计，模型仅在语言能降低预测误差时才会学习利用该信息。

Abstract: Pose histories provide the core kinematic evidence for 3D human motion prediction, but they lack explicit high-level semantic guidance. This paper introduces ZGL, a lightweight language-conditioned predictor that uses captions of the observed motion as a semantic prior while preserving a strong motion backbone as the main source of dynamics. We render only the observed poses, generate a one-sentence description with a vision-language model, encode the caption with a frozen CLIP-L text tower, and project it into a small set of conditioning tokens. These tokens are injected into a DCT-based spatial-temporal Transformer by compact crossattention adapters with zero gates: each adapter output is multiplied by a learnable gate initialized to zero, so the full network is numerically identical to the pose-only baseline at initialization and can learn to use language only when it reduces prediction error. On Human3.6M, ZGL improves overall MPJPE over representative motion-prediction baselines in our comparison. Results on CMUMocap further show that compact caption conditioning transfers to a second benchmark and provides a practical semantic cue for 3D human motion prediction.

</details>


### [88] [Again-Pose: Anchor-Guided Adaptive Inter-Frame Motion Cues Propagating for High-quality Human Pose Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29230)
*Shuaikang Zhu,Yiding Sun,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Again-Pose框架，通过锚点帧引导的运动线索传播，解决极端运动下视频3D人体姿态估计中特征坍塌导致的鲁棒性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式时序注意力聚合特征，但在严重运动模糊和遮挡下输入特征坍塌，隐式聚合无法区分有效信号，导致灾难性重建错误。

Method: 提出Again-Pose框架，基于特征显著性识别高质量锚点帧，显式传播可靠运动线索以"修复"退化中间帧的姿态。包含双路径运动感知模块（捕获帧间细粒度动态）和差分加权融合模块（自适应传播线索抑制漂移）。

Result: 在Human3.6M、3DPW、PoseTrack及FineDiving数据集上显著超越现有方法，在鲁棒性和稳定性上表现优异。

Conclusion: 将退化帧的姿态估计重新定义为运动引导的恢复任务，显式传播锚点帧的运动线索而非盲目平滑特征，有效解决了极端运动场景下的鲁棒性缺口。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous 3D human poses from unconstrained videos is challenging, especially in extreme motion scenarios involving severe motion blur and occlusion. Current state-of-the-art methods typically rely on implicit temporal attention to aggregate features across frames. However, under severe visual degradation, input features often suffer from collapse, rendering them indistinguishable from noise. In such cases, implicit aggregation fails to distinguish valid signals, leading to catastrophic reconstruction errors. To address this robustness gap, we propose a simple yet effective framework called Anchor-guided adaptive inter-frame motion cues propagating (Again-Pose), reformulating pose estimation in degraded frames as a motion-guided recovery task. Instead of blindly smoothing features, we explicitly identify high-quality Anchor Frames based on feature saliency and propagate reliable kinematic cues to "inpaint" the poses of degraded intermediate frames. Specifically, a Dual-path Motion-aware Module captures fine-grained inter-frame dynamics, while a Difference-weighted Fusion Module adaptively propagates these cues to suppress drift. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks (Human3.6M, 3DPW, PoseTrack) and the challenging FineDiving dataset demonstrate that Again-Pose significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in robustness and stability, effectively recovering plausible poses where other methods fail.

</details>


### [89] [When Does Synthetic CT Transfer? A Label-Free Donor/Host Diagnostic for Medical Vision-Language Model Routing on Real Lung CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29232)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需真实标签即可预测模型迁移能力的方法：通过合成数字孪生体，将模型能力分解为供体驱动（可迁移）和宿主驱动（不可迁移）两种成分，并在肺CT视觉语言任务的多个实验中得到验证（R²≥0.96）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像领域缺乏真实标注数据，导致无法直接验证模型在真实数据上的表现。研究者希望找到一种无需真实标签即可提前预测模型迁移能力的方法。

Method: 在合成数字孪生体上，将模型能力分解为供体驱动（结节自身属性）和宿主驱动（周围解剖结构属性）两种成分；通过分析模型在合成到真实数据迁移中哪些能力得以保留，预测迁移效果。

Result: 在三个肺CT视觉语言任务、五个公开VLM、四种引导条件和七个真实数据集上，供体驱动的能力（存在性和大小排序）迁移成功（R²≥0.96），而宿主驱动能力（肺叶定位）则无法迁移；该划分在留源校准中保持稳定，且边界在接触任何真实标签前即可确定。

Conclusion: 模型迁移能力本身是可预测的，无需真实标签，仅凭合成数据即可完成预测。TrialCouncil（仅在合成CT上校准的无训练路由）在预测可迁移的情况下与最优固定模型表现一致。

Abstract: A synthetic measurement of model competence is useful only if it survives the move to real data, yet the real labels that would verify it are exactly what medical imaging lacks. We ask whether transfer can be predicted in advance, label-free, and answer with a mechanism: on synthetic digital twins, competence that is donor-driven (a property of the transplanted nodule) survives the synthetic to real change of host, while host-driven competence (a property of the surrounding anatomy) need not. We test this on three lung CT vision-language tasks chosen to span that axis, across five public VLMs, four guidance conditions, and seven real datasets. The prediction holds in every case: presence and size orderings transfer (R2 >= 0.96), lobe does not; the split survives leave-source-out calibration, and the diagnostic names that boundary before any real label. TrialCouncil, a training-free council calibrated only on synthetic CT, confirms it by matching the best fixed model exactly where transfer is predicted. The contribution is not the router but the finding that transfer itself is predictable, label-free, from synthetic data alone.

</details>


### [90] [Confidence-feedback-weighted graph matching network: online-offline laser-induced damage site matching under complex interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29255)
*Yueyue Han,Guanhua Chen,Hangcheng Dong,Kang Zhang,Fengdong Chen,Zhitao Peng,Fa Zeng,Qihua Zhu,Guodong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对高功率激光装置光学元件在线检测中伪损伤点难以与真实损伤点匹配的问题，提出置信度反馈加权图匹配网络，仅需损伤点质心坐标作为输入，通过迭代反馈匹配置信度权重指导边特征聚合，结合几何一致性约束和难例挖掘损失，在复杂场景数据集上实现96.36%的匹配F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率激光装置光学元件的在线检测图像中存在与真实损伤高度相似的伪损伤点，导致难以判定在线检测点真实性，需要精确匹配到离线真值点。然而现有方法面临匹配判别特征有限、局部几何畸变和大量干扰点的挑战，且现有匹配模型主要通过损失函数隐式抑制干扰点。

Method: 提出置信度反馈加权图匹配网络：1) 仅需损伤点质心坐标作为输入；2) 从每轮匹配得分估计节点可匹配性置信度，反馈作为可靠性权重引导后续边特征聚合，抑制干扰传播并增强跨图判别性；3) 几何一致性约束校准虚假高置信度估计；4) 难例挖掘损失提升结构相似站点的辨别力。

Result: 在复杂场景数据集上，该方法匹配F1分数达到96.36%，表现出鲁棒且高效的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的置信度反馈加权图匹配网络有效解决了光学元件在线检测中伪损伤点与真损伤点的匹配难题，通过迭代置信度反馈机制显著提升了复杂场景下的匹配精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Online inspection images of final optics in high-power laser facilities contain pseudo-damage sites that closely resemble true damage sites. Determining the authenticity of online-detected sites is therefore difficult and requires accurate matching to offline ground-truth sites. However, this matching remains highly challenging due to limited match-discriminative features, local geometric distortions, and numerous distractor sites. Existing matching models mainly suppress distractors implicitly through loss-function supervision. We propose a confidence-feedback-weighted graph matching network that requires only damage-site centroid coordinates as input. It estimates node matchability confidence from each round of matching scores and feeds it back as a reliability weight to guide subsequent edge-feature aggregation, thereby suppressing distractor propagation and enhancing cross-graph discriminability. Within this framework, a geometric consistency constraint calibrates spurious high-confidence matchability estimates, while a hard-example mining loss improves discrimination between structurally similar sites. Experiments on our Complex-Scene dataset show that the proposed method achieves a matching F1-score of 96.36$\%$ with robust and efficient performance.

</details>


### [91] [Enhancing Part-Level Point Grounding for Any Open-Source MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29267)
*Jin-Cheng Jhang,Fu-En Wang,Xin Yang,Nan Qiao,Lu Xia,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用方法，通过冻结MLLM参数、利用注意力机制和轻量级Q-Synth模块与Attention-to-Point解码器，将任意开源多模态大模型扩展为具备精确2D部件级点定位能力，显著提升部件级指代表达理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在物体级视觉定位上表现良好，但在部件级定位上仍存在不足，而部件级定位是机器人操作等细粒度任务的核心需求，因此需要一种通用方案来增强MLLM的部件级点定位能力。

Method: 提出Q-Synth模块（在中间层合成文本条件化的、具有定位意识的查询）来捕获目标相关的注意力模式，再通过轻量级Attention-to-Point解码器将这些模式转换为点中心热图进行最终预测；所有原始MLLM参数冻结。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多个数据集上持续提升了部件级定位精度，且可无缝集成到任何开源MLLM中。

Conclusion: 该方法为MLLM提供了一种通用、高效、即插即用的部件级点定位增强方案，平衡了预训练能力的保持与细粒度定位性能的提升。

Abstract: Visual grounding aims to associate free-form textual queries with specific regions in an image. While recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in this domain, they primarily excel at object-level grounding and often struggle with part-level grounding-an essential requirement for fine-grained tasks such as robotic manipulation. In this work, we introduce a general approach that equips any open-source MLLMs with accurate 2D part-level point grounding, offering a more direct alternative to conventional grounding representations. Our method leverages the attention mechanisms inherently present in MLLMs. By synthesizing text-conditioned, grounding-aware queries within intermediate layers via the proposed Q-Synth Module, we capture target-relevant attention patterns and refine them with a lightweight Attention-to-Point Decoder, which converts these patterns into a point-centric heatmap for final prediction. Notably, all original MLLM parameters are frozen, ensuring full preservation of their pre-trained capabilities. Experiments show that our design consistently improves part-level grounding accuracy across datasets and can be seamlessly integrated into any open-source MLLMs.

</details>


### [92] [ScaleErasure: Inference-Time Minimal Intervention for Precise Concept Erasure in Next-Scale Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29282)
*Cong Wang,Haiyu Wu,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Fei Shen,Yafeng Yin,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleErasure 是一种针对下一尺度自回归图像生成模型的概念擦除方法，通过在推理时对跨尺度、令牌和位通道三个维度的 logits 进行精确选择与引导，在严重语义纠缠条件下有效擦除不安全概念，同时保持模型通用生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一尺度自回归图像生成范式中，早期尺度的语义信息高度压缩，导致不安全概念与无关语义严重纠缠，现有的概念擦除方法在此范式下效果不佳。

Method: ScaleErasure 在推理时进行两次额外前向传播（分别以不安全概念和对应安全概念为条件），利用输出引导目标 logits 远离不安全概念、靠近安全概念；并在尺度、令牌、位通道三个维度上进行 logits 选择与引导，实现精确的最小干预。

Result: 实验表明，ScaleErasure 在下一尺度自回归生成范式下优于改编的基线方法，能更精确地擦除不安全概念，同时较好地保留通用生成能力。

Conclusion: ScaleErasure 有效解决了下一尺度自回归生成中的概念擦除难题，为这一新兴生成范式的安全控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to prevent image generative models from producing unsafe content while preserving their general generative capability. Meanwhile, next-scale autoregressive (AR) image generation has recently emerged as a new generative paradigm characterized by next-scale prediction, for which concept erasure remains largely unexplored. In this paradigm, semantic information is highly compressed at early scales, leading to severe entanglement between unsafe and unrelated semantics. In this paper, we propose ScaleErasure, an inference-time concept erasure method that performs minimal intervention. ScaleErasure precisely selects and guides predicted logits that are most relevant to the unsafe concept, thereby enabling effective erasure under severe semantic entanglement. Specifically, ScaleErasure performs two additional forward passes conditioned on the unsafe concept and the corresponding safe concept, and leverages their outputs to guide the target logits away from unsafe concepts toward safe concepts. To enable precise and minimal intervention, logits selection and guidance are conducted across three dimensions: scales, tokens, and bit channels. Experiments demonstrate that ScaleErasure outperforms adapted baselines in the next-scale AR paradigm, achieving more precise concept erasure while largely preserving general generative capability. The code is available at https://github.com/coziiizz/ScaleErasure.

</details>


### [93] [ASTAD: Asymmetric Style Transfer for Synthetic-to-Real Adaptation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29286)
*Dingyi Yao,Xinqi Zhang,Lihui Peng,Jianming Hu,Danya Yao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称风格迁移任务（ASTAD），解决自动驾驶中合成数据有完美标注而真实数据无标签的实用不对称问题，并推出无需训练的两阶段框架ASTModel，实现语义一致的风格迁移，在下游感知任务中显著优于现有方法，推理速度提升3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据风格迁移方法忽略了实际中的不对称性：合成数据有完美像素级标注，而真实风格参考图像一般无对应标签。依赖对称语义引导的现有方法要么标注成本极高，要么存在严重语义错位。

Method: 提出ASTModel，一个无需训练的两阶段框架：第一阶段从无标签目标域提取粗糙语义先验；第二阶段在去噪过程中进行动态先验细化和类别一致风格注入，实现非对称约束下的域适应。

Result: ASTModel在下游感知任务效用和结构保真度上显著优于现有方法，同时提供3.2倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作使合成到真实域适应更贴合实际约束，具有加速鲁棒自动驾驶系统规模化部署的潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic data mitigates the data scarcity problem in autonomous driving perception. However, the synthetic-to-real gap leads to performance degradation, hindering real-world model generalization. Although current methods leverage diffusion models for photorealistic style transfer to bridge this gap, they critically ignore a practical asymmetry: while synthetic data possesses perfect pixel-level annotations, real-world style reference images generally lack corresponding labels. Consequently, existing methods relying on symmetric semantic guidance suffer from either prohibitive annotation costs or severe semantic misalignment. To address this dilemma, we formally propose a novel task: Asymmetric Style Transfer for Autonomous Driving (ASTAD), which requires semantically consistent transfer using only labeled synthetic content and unlabeled real-world references. We further introduce the ASTModel, a training-free two-stage framework designed to bridge this domain gap under asymmetric constraints. ASTModel first extracts a coarse semantic prior from the unlabeled target, followed by dynamic prior refinement and class-consistent style injection during the denoising process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASTModel significantly outperforms existing methods in downstream perception utility and structural fidelity, while offering a 3.2$\times$ inference speedup. This work aligns synthetic-to-real adaptation with practical constraints, holding the potential to accelerate the scalable deployment of robust autonomous driving systems. Code: https://github.com/Dingyi-Yao/ASTAD.

</details>


### [94] [Pointer-CAD v2: Plan-Then-Construct CAD Generation with Dimension-Aware Parametric Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29301)
*Dacheng Qi,Chenyu Wang,Jingwei Xu,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pointer-CAD v2提出了一种Plan-Then-Construct范式，通过解耦参数推理与几何构建，直接预测连续值而非量化参数，并利用指针机制引用结构化设计计划中的精确度量参数，从而消除量化误差，在CAD生成中实现了更高的几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成方法及评估协议主要关注视觉相似性，忽视了精确几何参数和正确度量尺度。小数值偏差虽在形状层面可忽略，但仍可能违反工业公差要求。此外，当前自回归范式采用命令序列表示并激进地量化数值参数以简化LLM预测，引入了量化误差。

Method: 提出Plan-Then-Construct统一框架：1）首先生成包含显式度量尺度参数的结构化设计计划，将参数组织为字典；2）通过指针机制在序列生成过程中直接引用这些参数，避免离散化误差；3）构建了带计划级注释的大规模新数据集，并引入三种层级几何精度度量（顶点、边、面级别）评估参数保真度。

Result: 大量实验表明Pointer-CAD v2在几何精度上持续优于现有基线方法，取得了显著改进，能够实现面向精度关键工程应用的可靠CAD生成。

Conclusion: 直接预测连续值并解耦参数推理与几何构建的范式有效消除了量化误差，提升了CAD生成的几何保真度，使其能够满足工业公差要求。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) plays a fundamental role in modern manufacturing by providing the high precision required for industrial production. Recent large language model based approaches formulate CAD generation as a sequence prediction problem and have achieved promising results. However, existing methods and evaluation protocols primarily emphasize visual similarity, while overlooking precise geometric parameters and correct metric scale. Small numerical deviations that are negligible at the shape-level may still violate industrial tolerance requirements, a problem further compounded by current autoregressive paradigms that utilize command sequence representations, aggressively quantize numerical parameters to ease LLM prediction. In this work, we present Pointer-CAD v2. Compared with v1 (arXiv:2603.04337), this version directly predicts continuous values, bypassing the need for quantized numerical parameters and thereby eliminating quantization errors. Specifically, we propose a unified framework that decouples parameter reasoning from geometric construction through a Plan-Then-Construct paradigm. Our method first produces a structured design plan with explicit metric scale parameters. These parameters are organized into a dictionary and directly referenced during sequence generation via a pointer mechanism, eliminating discretization errors and ensuring dimensionally consistent execution. In addition, we construct a new large-scale dataset with plan-level annotation and introduce three hierarchical geometry accuracy metrics to evaluate parametric fidelity at the vertex, edge, and face levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pointer-CAD v2 consistently outperforms existing baselines and achieves substantial improvements in geometric accuracy, enabling reliable CAD generation for precision-critical engineering applications.

</details>


### [95] [Occlusion-Robust Multi-Object Decoupling for Physics-Based Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29303)
*Xin Dong,Wenfeng Deng,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免掩码的无损多物体三维重建方法，从稀疏且带遮挡的真实视角出发，利用3D高斯泼溅与联合SDS过程实现物体解耦与几何补全，生成可直接用于MPM仿真的物理合理对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多物体重建中依赖标注掩码，且稀疏视角和遮挡导致物体耦合严重，难以获得完整、可仿真的几何结构。

Method: 以3D高斯泼溅为基础表示，用SAM2训练分割场获取粗粒度实例分区；通过联合SDS过程融合参考视图监督与新视角合成，结合2D和3D扩散先验保证纹理和三维一致性；引入物体内和物体间相似性等几何先验正则化几何推理。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上无需人工掩码即可生成完整且可直接用于仿真的3D物体，支持MPM模拟下的真实动态交互。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了稀疏遮挡视角下的多物体解耦与重建难题，避免了掩码依赖，生成的物体具备物理仿真可用性。

Abstract: We propose a mask-free method for lossless multi-object 3D reconstruction from sparse and occluded real-world views, enabling physically plausible interaction via Material Point Method (MPM) simulation. Our key insight is that object coupling stems from occlusion and limited viewpoints, which we address by formulating multi-object decoupling as a sparse-view reconstruction problem. Using 3D Gaussian Splatting as base representation, we first obtain coarse instance partitions with a SAM2-trained segmentation field. Rather than relying on masks, we reconstruct fragmented geometries by leveraging a joint Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) process, which integrates reference-view supervision with novel-view synthesis guided by 2D and 3D diffusion priors to enforce both texture fidelity and 3D consistency. Furthermore, we incorporate geometry-aware priors such as intra-object and inter-object similarity to regularize geometric reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that our method produces complete, simulation-ready 3D objects without requiring manual masks, enabling realistic dynamic interactions on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [96] [MirrorPPR: Exemplar-Based Portrait Photo Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29308)
*Zhihong Liu,Zheng Li,Jiachun Jin,Siqi Kou,Yitao Jian,Fengpei Yu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MirrorPPR框架，用于基于示例的结构化人像修饰。通过修饰操作提取器、连接器与LoRA模块注入预训练扩散Transformer，并构建了包含4700万配对数据的MirrorPPR47M数据集及自增强范式，在精细结构修饰上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导图像编辑难以描述面部特征和身体比例的精细结构变化，而现有的基于示例的编辑方法主要针对显著视觉变换任务，无法有效处理人像结构修饰中极其微妙和局部的修改。

Method: 1) 使用修饰操作提取器(Retouching Operation Extractor)捕获示例对的细微差异；2) 通过连接器和LoRA模块将提取的表示注入预训练扩散Transformer(DiT)；3) 提出数据自增强范式确保修饰操作严格对齐；4) 构建包含模拟和专业子集的4700万配对数据集MirrorPPR47M，支持渐进式课程学习。

Result: 大量实验表明MirrorPPR在修饰质量和身份保持方面显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于示例的人像结构修饰是一种有效的范式，MirrorPPR框架通过精细操作提取与注入、大规模数据集和自增强策略成功解决了该任务的核心挑战。

Abstract: While text-guided image editing has made remarkable progress, it remains limited in structural portrait retouching. Textual descriptions struggle to convey fine-grained changes to facial features and body proportions. To address this gap, we introduce Exemplar-Based Portrait Photo Retouching, where the model is given an exemplar pair and tasked with inferring and applying the same retouching operations to a new query image. Existing exemplar-based editing methods primarily focus on tasks with pronounced visual transformations. In contrast, structural portrait retouching involves extremely delicate and localized modifications, making accurate extraction and transfer of these edits challenging. To tackle this, we propose MirrorPPR, a novel framework designed to capture and transfer subtle structural retouching operations. Our method uses a Retouching Operation Extractor to capture the subtle differences from the exemplar pair. The extracted representations are then injected into a pre-trained Diffusion Transformer (DiT) through a connector and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules. Furthermore, constructing perfectly aligned cross-identity training pairs is severely hindered by operation misalignment. To overcome this, we propose an advanced data self-augmentation paradigm that ensures strictly aligned retouching operations. To alleviate data scarcity and support this novel task, we introduce MirrorPPR47M, a large-scale dataset with over 47 million retouched pairs. By structuring the dataset into simulated and professional subsets, we enable progressive curriculum learning to smoothly optimize the network. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MirrorPPR significantly outperforms existing baselines in both retouching quality and identity preservation. The project page is available at https://sjtu-deng-lab.github.io/MirrorPPR.

</details>


### [97] [D$^{2}$R$^{2}$OSR: Degradation-Disentangled Representation for Real-World Omnidirectional Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29314)
*Hongyu An,Xinfeng Zhang,Xu Fan,Shijie Zhao,Li Zhang,Ruiqin Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D²R²OSR框架，通过解耦鱼眼成像和ERP投影退化，结合视角感知的透视投影表示与退化专用模块，实现全景图像的真实世界超分，在保持计算效率的同时达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实超分方法无法处理全景图像中鱼眼捕获和ERP投影引入的复杂退化（如混叠和投影畸变），导致360°细节渲染不足。

Method: 提出退化解耦表示框架D²R²OSR：(1) 引入透视投影表示(PPR)与ERP分支并行，捕获视角感知特征；(2) 设计退化专用模块(DSM)联合建模ERP几何畸变和PPR真实退化。

Result: 在多项实验上达到SOTA性能，生成高保真高质量的全景超分结果，同时保持较低计算开销支持低资源部署。

Conclusion: 投影先验对真实退化建模至关重要，视角中心的感知特性有效指导了全景超分任务的设计。

Abstract: With the growing demand for immersive visual experiences, high-quality omnidirectional images (ODIs) have become increasingly important. However, limitations in imaging devices and transmission bandwidth often lead to low-resolution ODIs, hindering the rendering of fine-grained 360° details, especially in the presence of real-world degradations and geometric distortions. Existing real-world super-resolution (Real-SR) methods are inadequate for ODIs, as their degradation models fail to account for the complex imaging pipeline involving fisheye capture and Equirectangular Projection (ERP), introducing severe aliasing and projection-specific distortions. To address these challenges, we propose D$^{2}$R$^{2}$OSR, a Degradation-Disentangled Representation framework for Real-world Omnidirectional image Super-Resolution. D$^{2}$R$^{2}$OSR explicitly models degradations arising from both fisheye imaging and ERP projection, guided by two key insights: (1) projection priors play a critical role in shaping real-world degradations, and (2) human perception in immersive environments is inherently viewpoint-centric. Accordingly, we introduce a Perspective Projection Representation (PPR) operating alongside the ERP branch to capture viewpoint-aware features, together with a Degradation-Specific Module (DSM) that jointly models ERP-induced geometric distortions and PPR-specific real-world degradations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that D$^{2}$R$^{2}$OSR achieves state-of-the-art performance and produces visually compelling, high-fidelity omnidirectional Real-SR results while maintaining favorable computational efficiency for low-resource deployment.

</details>


### [98] [FDM-MFVT: Few-step Sampling Diffusion Model for Mask-Free Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29319)
*Jiaxin Liu,Xiaoye Liang,Lai Jiang,Mai Xu,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FDM-MFVT，一种仅需6步采样的少步扩散模型用于无掩膜虚拟试穿，结合噪声优化模块和指令驱动模块，并引入3万对的无掩膜数据集MFVT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的虚拟试穿方法需要大量采样步数和昂贵辅助网络生成的掩膜，且缺乏大规模无掩膜配对数据集，限制了无掩膜虚拟试穿的发展。

Method: 提出FDM-MFVT，包含两个核心模块：(1) OANO模块利用输入图像初始化带噪对齐空间，仅需6步即可生成高保真试穿图像；(2) IDT模块通过虚拟试穿提示和高效适配，仅从服装和人物图像生成高质量结果。同时构建了包含30,000对的MFVT无掩膜数据集。

Result: 在定量和定性评估中，FDM-MFVT以更少的推理步数优于基于掩膜和无掩膜的基线方法。

Conclusion: FDM-MFVT通过少步扩散模型实现了高效、灵活的无掩膜虚拟试穿，解决了现有方法依赖掩膜和多步采样的局限。

Abstract: Image-based Virtual Try-On (IVTON) has greatly advanced through diffusion models, yet existing methods require many sampling steps and depend on masks with costly auxiliary networks. In addition, the absence of large-scale mask-free paired datasets further limits the development of mask-free IVTON. We propose FDM-MFVT, a few-step diffusion model for mask-free IVTON, integrating an Outfit-aware Noise Optimization Module (OANO) and an Instruction-driven Try-on Module (IDT) to enhance efficiency and flexibility.The OANO module initializes the alignment space with noise using the input image and only needs 6 steps to generate a higher-fidelity try-on image compared to 30 steps.The IDT module uses virtual try-on prompts and efficient adaptation to generate high-quality results from garment and person images alone. We further introduce MFVT, a 30,000-pair mask-free IVTON dataset. Experiments show that FDM-MFVT achieves superior quantitative and qualitative results with fewer inference steps than mask-based and mask-free baseline methods.

</details>


### [99] [RAGA: Real Time Ray Traced Gaussian Shadow Casting for 3DGS Avatar-Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29329)
*Aymen Mir,Riza Alp Guler,Jian Wang,Peter Wonka,Bing Zhou,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAGA是一种基于光线追踪的高斯阴影投射方法，利用精确的射线-高斯线积分实现3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）场景中的物理合理阴影计算，无需网格重建，支持单人、多人及物体交互场景，运行速度约50 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS化身动画中的阴影生成方法依赖二值命中测试和基于网格的阴影投射器，无法在纯高斯空间中实现物理合理的阴影计算，导致阴影真实感不足且时间稳定性差。

Method: 基于精确的射线-高斯线积分，沿阴影射线积分每个遮挡高斯的不透明度轮廓并用理论最大积分归一化，得到反映射线穿过遮挡物方式的权重；引入化身代理表示以减少动画变形带来的时间方差；使用定制CUDA内核集成NVIDIA OptiX框架实现。

Result: 在单人、多人及化身-物体交互场景中，相比现有方法，阴影真实感、时间稳定性和场景一致性显著提升，运行速度约50 FPS。

Conclusion: RAGA在纯高斯空间中实现了高效且物理合理的阴影投射，无需网格重建，为3DGS场景中的动态化身阴影生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of physically plausible shadow casting when animating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) avatars, either individually or in multi-avatar and object-interaction scenarios, within existing 3DGS scenes. In contrast to prior methods that rely on binary hit tests and mesh-based shadow casters, our method performs shadow computation entirely in Gaussian space, without requiring any mesh reconstruction. We introduce RAGA, a Ray-Traced Gaussian Shadow Casting formulation based on exact ray-Gaussian line integrals. For each occluding Gaussian, we integrate the opacity profile along the shadow ray and normalize by the theoretical maximum integral, producing a weight that captures how the ray traverses the occluder rather than merely whether an intersection occurred. To reduce temporal variance from clothing deformations in animated avatars, we further introduce an avatar proxy representation that stabilizes shadow casting while preserving visual fidelity. We implement RAGA using custom CUDA kernels integrated with the NVIDIA OptiX framework; as such, our shadow tracer runs at rates of about 50 FPS. We evaluate on single-avatar, multi-avatar, and avatar-object interaction scenarios across multiple datasets, demonstrating substantially improved shadow realism, temporal stability, and scene coherence. Our project page is available at https://miraymen.github.io/raga/.

</details>


### [100] [HiReFF: High-Resolution Feedforward Human Reconstruction from Uncalibrated Sparse-View Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29333)
*Yiming Jiang,Hanzhang Tu,Wenfeng Song,Siyou Lin,Liang An,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiReFF是一种前馈式方法，可从无标定的稀疏视角视频（四视角，间隔90°）实现2K分辨率360°人体视频重建，通过尺度同步相机标定、高斯级前景掩码和高分辨率侧调谐技术，在保持计算高效性的同时显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未标定体积视频流方法依赖逐场景优化或标定相机，而前馈模型仅能实现低分辨率（0.5K）单帧合成，缺乏时间一致性和计算效率，无法满足高分辨率全息通信和AR/VR的需求。

Method: 将问题分解为两个任务：1) 前景3D高斯重建，采用尺度同步相机标定解决尺度模糊性，高斯级前景掩码通过调节高斯参数重建干净前景；2) 高效高分辨率合成，通过高分辨率侧调谐在保持0.5K主干网络的同时附加补充特征实现2K渲染，极大降低计算开销。

Result: HiReFF在高分辨率流式体积视频重建中显著优于现有方法，实现了从稀疏视角输入到2K 360°人体视频的稳定重建。

Conclusion: HiReFF首次实现了从无标定稀疏视频输入到2K分辨率360°人体视频的前馈式重建，为高保真全息通信和AR/VR应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Uncalibrated volumetric video streaming for human reconstruction is essential for holographic communication and AR/VR, yet remains challenging due to the need for temporal consistency and computational efficiency from sparse-view inputs. Existing methods rely on per-scene optimization or calibrated cameras, while recent feed-forward models are limited to low-resolution (0.5K) single-frame synthesis. We present HiReFF, a feed-forward method for 2K-resolution 360° human video reconstruction from uncalibrated sparse-view videos. Our framework decomposes the problem into two key tasks: foreground 3D Gaussian reconstruction from sparse-view videos (four views separated by 90°) and computationally efficient high-resolution synthesis. To enable the former, we propose Scale-synchronized Camera Calibration to resolve scale ambiguity for multi-view supervision, and Gaussian-wise Foreground Masking to reconstruct clean foregrounds by modulating Gaussian parameters. For efficient high-resolution synthesis, our High-resolution Side-tuning achieves 2K rendering by augmenting the Gaussian head with supplementary features while keeping the backbone at 0.5K, drastically reducing computational overhead. Experiments demonstrate that HiReFF significantly outperforms existing methods in high-resolution streaming volumetric video reconstruction. https://iridescentjiang.github.io/HiReFF

</details>


### [101] [Multi-scale Object-Aware Gaze Estimation via Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29334)
*Jiajie Mi,Xinyu Liu,Mengke Song,Chenglizhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对象语义的两阶段注视目标估计框架，将注视估计重构为分层推理过程，通过引入对象级特征编码、多尺度融合和头部姿态几何约束，在四个基准数据集上取得领先性能（AUC最高达0.987），且参数量仅7.1M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将注视目标估计建模为从全局特征到注视热图的直接映射（像素级回归），未能将注视对象表示为独立实体，导致复杂场景下预测不稳定且语义不一致。作者希望显式建模注视对象的语义实体特性，提升预测的鲁棒性和语义一致性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段在特征编码中引入对象级表征，将图像特征与离散语义实体对齐；第二阶段引入多尺度特征融合以及来自头部姿态和注视方向的几何约束，实现细粒度定位和对象级判别。

Result: 在GazeFollow、VideoAttentionTarget、ChildPlay和GOO-Real上AUC分别达到0.961、0.948、0.987和0.977，参数量仅7.1M，在多个基准上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过将注视目标估计重构为对象语义引导的分层推理过程，显式建模注视对象的实体性，能在复杂场景中产生稳定且语义一致的预测，同时保持模型轻量化。

Abstract: Gaze target estimation aims to predict the semantic object an observer fixates upon within an image, a task deeply rooted in the object-oriented nature of human gaze. Observers tend to select a specific semantic entity as the attentional target, rather than responding randomly across arbitrary regions of the image. However, existing methods typically model this task as a direct mapping from global features to gaze heatmaps, essentially treating it as a pixel-level regression problem. This approach fails to explicitly represent the gazed object as a distinct entity, making it difficult to produce stable and semantically consistent predictions in complex scenes. To address this, we propose a two-stage gaze estimation framework guided by object semantics, reformulating gaze target estimation as a hierarchical reasoning process. Our method incorporates object-level representations during feature encoding to align image features with discrete semantic entities, then introduces multi-scale feature fusion and geometric constraints from head pose and gaze direction for fine-grained localization and object-level discrimination. Extensive experiments on GazeFollow, VideoAttentionTarget, ChildPlay, and GOO-Real demonstrate that our method achieves AUC of 0.961, 0.948, 0.987, and 0.977 respectively, delivering strong performance across all benchmarks while maintaining a compact parameter size of 7.1M.

</details>


### [102] [W4A4 Quantization for Inference on Wan2.2-I2V-A14B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29337)
*Yidong Chen,Chengyu Shi,Jiahao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Wan-AI/Wan2.2-I2V-A14B模型在HiF4/MXFP4格式下的W4A4量化方案，结合MixQ风格混合精度处理激活异常值、SmoothQuant风格逐通道平滑以及分块HiF4打包技术，在VBench指标上相比FP16仅下降2-3.5%，优于原生HiFloat4基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模图像到视频（I2V）生成模型Wan2.2在4-bit权重量化和4-bit激活量化（W4A4）下的推理精度退化问题，特别是应对激活层中存在的重尾异常值通道导致的量化困难。

Method: 1) MixQ风格混合精度：识别稀疏激活异常值并以更高精度保留；2) SmoothQuant风格逐通道平滑：在校准阶段重新平衡动态范围；3) 双分支GEMM：异常值列保持高精度，主体通道严格使用W4A4；4) 分块HiF4打包：针对前馈线性层进行块级量化格式实现。

Result: 在官方VBench I2V指标上，所提方法在大多数质量轴上仅偏离FP16结果2-3.5%，并提升了运动平滑度，优于原生HiFloat4基线（该基线在所有报告分数上相对FP16退化约5%）。

Conclusion: 结合混合精度异常值处理与平滑量化策略能有效缓解W4A4量化对视频生成模型的质量损失，为低比特大模型推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We summarize our submission to Sub-Challenge 1: W4A4 Quantization for Inference (HiF4 / MXFP4) of the ICME 2026 Low-Bit-width Large-Model Quantization Challenge. The sub-challenge targets 4-bit weight and 4-bit activation inference on Wan-AI/Wan2.2-I2V-A14B under HiF4 or MXFP4 numerical formats. We adapt two complementary ideas from LLM quantization, MixQ-style mixed precision for sparse activation outliers and SmoothQuant-style per-channel smoothing, together with block-wise HiF4 packing for Wan2.2 feed-forward linear layers. Calibration on representative OpenS2V-5M batches identifies heavy-tailed activation channels; smoothing rebalances dynamic range before W4A4 rounding; and a dual-branch GEMM preserves outlier columns in higher precision while the bulk of channels use strict W4A4. On official VBench I2V metrics, our pipeline stays within 2-3.5 percent of FP16 on most quality axes and improves motion smoothness, outperforming a native HiFloat4 baseline that degrades roughly 5 percent relative to FP16 across all reported scores.

</details>


### [103] [Fast Enough to Act: Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Merging for Low-Latency Robotic VLMs and VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29350)
*Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang,Gang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ST-Merge 是一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过在视觉编码阶段融合冗余 tokens，实现 2~8.3 倍的推理加速，同时仅带来微小精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型和视觉语言动作模型在处理视频和高分辨率图像时产生大量视觉 tokens，导致推理延迟极高，严重阻碍机器人实时控制。为突破这一计算瓶颈，提出 ST-Merge。

Method: 1) 显式构建3D时空坐标；2) 采用多队列并行匹配与加权聚合机制，跨帧高效融合冗余 tokens；3) 引入合并后位置校正机制（Post-Merge Positional Correction），通过动态重新评估加权质心的旋转位置编码，消除空间偏差。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL 上视频问答任务实现 2 倍加速，精度仅下降 1%；在 π_{0.5} VLA 策略上，1024×1024 分辨率下实现 8.3 倍加速且成功率持平基线。

Conclusion: ST-Merge 有效解决了多帧视觉输入的计算瓶颈，在保持高精度空间感知的前提下大幅提升推理速度，适用于机器人实时控制场景。

Abstract: Vision-language models and vision-language action models endow the robot with unprecedented capabilities. However, the input of video and high-resolution images yields a massive number of visual tokens, leading to extremely high inference latency and severely hindering the robot's real-time control. To break through this computational bottleneck, we propose ST-Merge, a plug-and-play, training-free framework that efficiently fuses redundant tokens directly during the visual encoding phase. By explicitly constructing 3D spatiotemporal coordinates, it employs a multi-queue parallel matching and weighted aggregation mechanism to achieve efficient and geometrically consistent fusion of redundant tokens across frames. In addition, we introduce a post-merge positional correction mechanism that effectively eliminates spatial deviation caused by merging by dynamically re-evaluating the rotational position code of the weighted centroid of the vision token, thereby ensuring the high-precision spatial awareness required for dexterous operation. In the Video Question Answering task on the mainstream VLM, Qwen2.5-VL, ST-Merge achieves a 2$\times$ inference speedup with only a tiny 1\% loss in precision. When deployed on the $π_{0.5}$ VLA policy, ST-Merge achieves an 8.3$\times$ speedup at 1024 $\times$ 1024 resolution and matches the baseline success rate at this high-resolution setting. At lower resolutions, it introduces a small drop in accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamic Parsing and Updating Natural Language Specification using VLMs for Robust Vision-Language Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29357)
*Xiao Wang,Liye Jin,Dan Xu,Yuehang Li,Lan Chen,Yaowei Wang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言依赖解析机制，通过提取核心跟踪主成分（目标对象、语义概念、背景上下文），并利用Qwen-VL模型实现组件感知的自适应文本描述更新，在多个视觉-语言跟踪基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言跟踪方法虽能通过自适应优化文本描述缓解语义-视觉不匹配问题，但主流方法（序列模型或大语言模型直接生成文本）存在目标更新错误、背景干扰过度和幻觉伪影等固有缺陷。

Method: 提出语言依赖解析机制，精确提取核心跟踪主成分（目标对象、语义概念、背景上下文）；基于Qwen-VL预训练视觉-语言模型的跨模态理解能力，执行组件感知的自适应文本描述更新。

Result: 在TNL2K、LaSOT、TNLLT和OTB-LANG等多个大规模视觉-语言跟踪基准上，集成所提模块的基线方法取得了一致且优越的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的语言依赖解析与组件感知自适应更新机制有效克服了主流方法的缺陷，显著提升了视觉-语言跟踪的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language tracking guided by natural language specifications leverages high-level semantic cues of target objects to substantially boost tracking accuracy and robustness. Existing studies have verified that adaptively optimizing textual descriptions throughout the tracking process can effectively mitigate the semantic-visual mismatch induced by dynamic variations in target appearance, position, and other inherent attributes. Nevertheless, mainstream methods that directly generate textual information via sequence models or large language models inevitably suffer from inherent defects, including erroneous target updating, excessive background distraction, and pervasive hallucination artifacts. To address the aforementioned limitations, this paper proposes a novel language dependency parsing mechanism to precisely distill core tracking principal components, encompassing target objects, semantic concepts, and background contextual information. On this basis, we perform component-aware adaptive textual description updates by exploiting the powerful cross-modal understanding capability of the pre-trained vision-language model Qwen-VL. By integrating the proposed elaborately designed modules into the baseline framework, our method achieves consistent and superior tracking performance on multiple large-scale vision-language tracking benchmarks, including TNL2K, LaSOT, TNLLT, and OTB-LANG. The source code and pre-trained models will be released at https://github.com/Event-AHU/Open_VLTrack.

</details>


### [105] [SAFE-DiT: Semantics-Aware Fast-path Execution for High-Resolution Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29360)
*Xuanhua Yin,Yuxuan Jia,Chuanzhi Xu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAFE-DiT提出了一种无需训练的高分辨率扩散Transformer推理加速框架，通过识别并消除冗余的空间自注意力掩码（避免FlashAttention的加速路径退化），结合语义感知的令牌分区和选择性状态更新，在Lumina-Next上实现最高5.09倍端到端加速，并大幅降低显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率DiT推理中存在大量空间冗余，但现有空间自适应实现将区域计算编码为注意力掩码，导致SDPA偏离FlashAttention快速路径（即Mask-Induced Dispatch Tax, MIDT），且该瓶颈随序列长度增长而加剧。

Method: SAFE-DiT框架包含两个核心部分：(1) SAFE-Core——直接移除经证明仅产生逐行常偏移的图片自注意力掩码，保留语义相关掩码（如文本填充掩码），通过提示条件化的令牌分区、选择性状态更新和周期性上下文刷新实现空间适应；(2) SAFE-DiT+SW——引入敏感性加权的无分类器引导，作为独立的提示对齐操作点。

Result: 冗余掩码使长序列注意力比无掩码路径慢4.1-5.8倍；在Lumina-Next上，SAFE-DiT在1024^2分辨率实现2.69倍加速，2560^2实现5.09倍加速；峰值显存从94.1降至27.9 GB；支持3072^2生成（密集推理OOM）；配对指标、消融实验和盲人研究支持视觉非劣效性。

Conclusion: 通过精确掩码消除与近似空间调度解耦，实现了免训练的高效DiT推理加速，在显著提升速度和降低显存的同时保持视觉质量，为高分辨率扩散模型的系统优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: High-resolution Diffusion Transformer (DiT) inference contains substantial spatial redundancy, but many spatially adaptive implementations encode regional computation as attention masks, which can inadvertently move scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) away from FlashAttention fast paths. We identify this avoidable systems bottleneck as Mask-Induced Dispatch Tax (MIDT) and show that it grows with latent sequence length. We introduce SAFE-DiT, a training-free Semantics-Aware Fast-path Execution framework that separates exact mask elision from approximation-based spatial scheduling. SAFE-DiT removes only provenance-certified image self-attention masks that induce a row-wise constant shift in attention logits, preserves semantics-bearing masks such as text-padding masks, and realizes spatial adaptation through prompt-conditioned token partitioning, selective state updates with global context, and periodic context refresh. We call this acceleration-only configuration SAFE-Core and report sensitivity-weighted classifier-free guidance separately as SAFE-DiT+SW. On the evaluated PyTorch SDPA stack, redundant masks make long-sequence attention $4.1\times$ to $5.8\times$ slower than the mask-free path. On Lumina-Next, SAFE-DiT achieves $2.69\times$ end-to-end acceleration at $1024^2$ resolution and $5.09\times$ at $2560^2$, reduces peak memory at $2560^2$ from 94.1 to 27.9 GB, and enables $3072^2$ generation when dense inference runs out of memory. Paired metrics, component ablations, and a blinded human study support visual non-inferiority of SAFE-Core to the dense fast-path baseline, while SAFE-DiT+SW provides a separate prompt-alignment operating point without reintroducing spatial self-attention masks. Code is available at https://github.com/xuanhuayin/SAFE-DiT.

</details>


### [106] [L2D2-GS: Learning to Densify for Feedforward Dynamic Gaussian Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29374)
*Zetian Song,Chenming Wu,Junnan Liu,Chitian Sun,Liangliang He,Hangjun Ye,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Wen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: L2D2-GS提出了一种统一的框架，将可泛化的动态城市场景重建从单次回归重构为鲁棒的迭代优化与致密化过程，通过自监督致密化策略和几何正则化机制，在减少高斯原语数量的同时实现了SOTA的重建保真度和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法依赖昂贵的逐场景优化，难以扩展；前馈式方法虽然速度快，但在高分辨率下内存开销大且难以一致地融合密集多视角观测。本文旨在解决这些瓶颈，实现高效且可泛化的动态城市场景重建。

Method: 1) 将可泛化重建重构为迭代优化与致密化过程，而非一次性回归；2) 提出自监督致密化策略，从全局重建收益中导出显式奖励信号以指导局部致密化；3) 引入几何正则化机制，利用重参数化约束优化流形，防止陷入不良局部最优。

Result: 在PandaSet和Waymo数据集上，该方法在重建保真度和零样本泛化方面达到SOTA，且使用的原语数量少于竞争基线。

Conclusion: L2D2-GS通过迭代优化与自监督致密化的设计，有效平衡了效率与保真度，为动态城市场景的可泛化重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity reconstruction of dynamic urban environments is a cornerstone of autonomous driving simulation and large-scale world modeling. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has established a new standard for real-time rendering, its reliance on expensive per-scene optimization limits scalability. Conversely, recent feedforward methods that infer Gaussian parameters offer faster speed but face fundamental bottlenecks: they are memory-prohibitive at high resolutions and struggle to fuse dense multi-view observations consistently. This paper presents L2D2-GS, a unified framework that reformulates generalizable reconstruction not as a one-shot regression, but as a robust iterative process of optimization and densification. To resolve the ambiguity of supervision in primitive generation, we propose a self-supervised densification policy that derives explicit reward signals from global reconstruction gains to guide local densification. Furthermore, we mitigate irreversible early-stage artifacts through a geometric regularization mechanism, utilizing reparameterization to constrain the optimization manifold and prevent convergence to poor local optima. Extensive experiments on the PandaSet and Waymo datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity and strong zero-shot generalization, while using fewer primitives than competing baselines.

</details>


### [107] [SAD-GS: Learning Reliable 3D Semantic Gaussian Fields via Dynamic Geo-Semantic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29376)
*Yufei Zhang,Chenlu Zhan,Gaoang Wang,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAD-GS，通过动态地理语义锚定（语义锚蒸馏+地理语义反馈循环）解决多视图2D监督中语义漂移和掩码错误问题，在开放词汇定位和语义分割任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D语义高斯场学习依赖多视图2D监督，但2D目标存在视角相关特征导致的语义身份漂移、传播跟踪掩码带来的边界泄露和身份切换等问题，直接优化会导致3D表示吸收多视图矛盾，产生严重误差累积。

Method: 提出SAD-GS框架：1）语义锚蒸馏（SAD）将每视图视觉嵌入蒸馏到共识文本锚点，建立视角不变的语义身份；2）地理语义反馈循环（GSFL）利用演化中的3D场主动过滤跟踪异常，通过保守的三门更新规则细化空间掩码分配。

Result: 在LERF-OVS、3D-OVS和Mip-NeRF360数据集上，SAD-GS在开放词汇定位和语义分割任务中均取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 动态地理语义锚定能够有效提升3D语义高斯场学习的可靠性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D semantic Gaussian field learning relies on multi-view 2D supervision, whose semantic targets and spatial assignments are often unreliable. Across varying viewpoints, view-dependent features cause semantic identity drift, while propagated tracker masks introduce boundary leakage and identity switches. Directly optimizing against these unreliable 2D targets forces the 3D representation to absorb multi-view contradictions, leading to severe error accumulation. To resolve this limitation, we propose SAD-GS, a framework for learning reliable 3D semantic Gaussian fields via dynamic geo-semantic anchoring. Specifically, Semantic Anchor Distillation (SAD) distills per-view visual embeddings into consensus text anchors to establish a viewpoint-invariant semantic identity. Concurrently, the Geo-Semantic Feedback Loop (GSFL) leverages the evolving 3D field to actively filter tracker anomalies and refine spatial mask assignments via a conservative three-gate update rule. Extensive evaluations on LERF-OVS, 3D-OVS, and Mip-NeRF360 show that SAD-GS consistently achieves the best overall performance in both open-vocabulary localization and semantic segmentation. These comprehensive improvements validate the effectiveness and robustness of dynamic geo-semantic anchoring for reliable 3D semantic Gaussian field learning.

</details>


### [108] [DR-GS: Physically-Based Deformable and Relightable 2D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29379)
*Jiaxin Li,Tong Wu,Yi Wei,Tailin Wu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DR-GS框架，通过解耦几何、光照和材质表示，实现可变形物体的物理一致渲染、重光照和后期材质编辑，克服了现有高斯泼溅方法中光照烘焙到纹理和无法编辑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的可变形物体方法存在两个关键局限：(1)光照被错误地烘焙到纹理中，导致在动态变形和光照变化下产生物理不一致的响应；(2)基于快照的重建限制了后期材质编辑能力。

Method: 提出DR-GS（可变形且可重光照的高斯泼溅）框架，是一个统一的高斯框架，集成了基于物理的逆渲染、重光照和变形感知操作。核心是通过显式解耦几何、光照和材质表示来克服静态快照的限制。
